_Roughly 14:53-20:30 is an all-English planning talk (AI tools, tests); 12:27-13:25, 20:31-30:23, 36:37-42:59 and 45:18-1:00:46 are Latin-transliterated, often garbled (e.g. 25:05-25:38 'ana bihna binkhales al-asal', 'ahana shay na'na' ma asal mufrad' could not be parsed into rows). 22:49-24:55 is a honey seller at the door (not Amal). Speaker labels are fragmented; many '?' lines were assigned from flow (e.g. 02:31 'or يتعلّم', 34:00 'هو بتيبست' self-fixed). The lesson is one long drill on بسّط/انبسط conjugations, so grammar rows dominate and vocab rows are few. Left out as S4 pronunciation: لقة/luka for لغة (00:39), بسّتني for بسطني (30:35), بيسبت for بيبسط (32:30), بنسبت for بنبسط (34:21), bansebet for banbeset (56:04), بيسبط for ببسّط (1:02:23), maruj for mahruj (22:09), 'ma' for ma3 (25:38, 26:19), stress on the T (55:25). English words inside Arabic not on her sheet, so not tier 3: software, research, possibility, test (she uses 'test' herself), mispronounce, AI. 26:19 'aswad' read as aswa2 = worse (on sheet), so not a vocab error. / Diarization fragmented: many Medi lines split across '?' fragments and some Amal/Medi labels swapped (e.g. 09:34-09:38 'Al AI مرات مش ناجح' labelled Amal is Medi). ### 2026-09-18

_Lines 1-90 are Arabic script; from ~05:00 the transcript is Latin transliteration. 19:20-31:30 has many Medi/Amal Arabic words dropped entirely (blank gaps: 'So can, can', 'Is it or-', 'be as'), so the flight/meetings/parents/'I made him smile' drills could not be checked and rows 20-21 are low confidence. Speaker labels look right; a few Amal echo lines (15:49 'Leesh zehekti', 46:19 'Saw') may be flattened. Not written as errors: pronunciation/vowel repeats (01:52 أتمارنت, 04:27 أزرق, 09:13 kuffet->khuffet, 46:13 sawa->sawwa, 53:44 and 58:42 double-T commands), factual time fixes at 03:41-04:11 (2 o'clock, 26 min), his own self-fix 03:02 مختلفة, listening comprehension 56:07 him/her, and his unfinished 1:00:54 'il film mbare' which she completed in chat at 1:04:26. medi_turns counts every Medi line with an Arabic attempt (script or Latin), excluding pure filler. / Arabic script only for 00:13-04:53; from 05:00 to the end the engine wrote Latin transliteration, and between roughly 19:30 and 31:30 it dropped many of Medi's and Amal's Arabic words entirely (lines like 'So is it ?', 'Is [ ] I am tired'), so several fixes there (show made me laugh, work made me tired, he's funny, he makes me bored, I made him smile, meetings make me bored, my parents made me upset, the flight made me tired) can only be inferred from Amal's English prompts and her later chat lines; those rows are marked low. Speaker labels looked right throughout. _First ~6.5 min is English chat about a song; her Arabic there is redacted as [foreign language] so the balad/bilad and 'sakit/yard' questions cannot be quoted in Arabic. Speaking Arabic is limited to 06:26-10:40 (date, time, weather, clothes) plus a few Arabic productions inside the drill (38:13 ma tahammasi, 41:10 inti kharabti il safra - correct, 44:46 bored->زهقان, 1:00:47 ahsan katir iliyom - correct). From 11:13 to 1:00:35 the lesson is a listening drill: Amal says a verb sentence, he translates to English; misreads are logged with mode=listening (rows 14-36); correctly translated sentences are not logged. Pronunciation-only slips left out per S4: خندرة for كندرة (10:31, chat kundara), بيكميس for قميص (10:08), Shufik for أشوفك (1:02:02), Kamara/Tawamara for 'كمان مرة'. Trailing CHAT lines 1:02:22-1:04:26 are transcriptions of drill sentences, not fixes. medi_turns = 37 Medi turns with Arabic script + 12 Arabizi/Latin Arabic turns; ~55 further Medi turns are English translations in the listening drill. / 00:00-06:26 is English chat about a song; Amal's Arabic there is redacted as [foreign language], so the 03:22-03:57 words (loaf, yard) cannot be recovered - only two asked-rows written from his Latin (balad/bilad, Sakit). 06:26-10:49 is the only speaking stretch (date, time, weather, clothes). 11:13-1:00:35 is a listening drill: Amal says a sentence, Medi gives the English - so most rows are mode=listening and 'wrong' is his English reading.
